Stage 2 manual audit -- full posting text for reading (200 postings)
O.K. Samarasinghe -- COBSCCOMP242P-069

This file is a READING COPY ONLY. It does not replace
src/audit/stage2_coding_tool.py -- you still enter your Y/N/PARTIAL/NA
judgements through that tool, which saves them to the worksheet.
Use this file if it's easier to read on another device, print, or
scroll through without the terminal's 400-char snippet truncation.

The true fraudulent/legitimate label is deliberately left out of this
file, same as the coding tool's default -- seeing it while you read
would bias your own judgement (see decisions.md, blinding rationale).

S004  |  Overnight Personal Assistant (For 2 Fun-Loving Women with Disabilities)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
company_profile: <h3>United Cerebral Palsy of Oregon &amp; SW Washington (UCP) supports adults with all kinds of developmental disabilities (not just cerebral palsy), so that they can live the life of their dreams.</h3>
<h3><b><i>We're growing, so you will see lots of exciting job openings in the next few months!</i></b></h3>
<p></p>
<h3>WHY WORK FOR UCP?</h3>
<ul>
<li>
<b>As an employee of UCP, you'll be proud of the work you do, every day! </b>Our employees assist the people we support to live independently in their own homes and apartments, find and keep their dream jobs, make community connections, serve as their own advocates, and make their own choices. </li>
<li>
<b>You'll get the chance to work for one of the 100 Best Non-Profit Employers in Oregon. </b>We're fun and flexible, and there's plenty of room for advancement and growth.</li>
</ul><p></p>

description:
<p>Are you looking for overnight work and the chance to make a real difference? Then United Cerebral Palsy (UCP) might have the perfect job for you!</p>
<p>We are currently seeking a Full-Time Personal Assistant who will provide in-home support to two fun-loving and independent women who experience disabilities, assisting them to live in their own homes and pursue the life of their dreams. </p>
<p>This is a great job for students, because you’ll get to make a big difference, and will be paid to sleep (however, you will need to get up in the night 1-2 times per week on average). Please note that female applicants are strongly preferred.</p>
<p>You will spend two nights a week supporting a woman who lives in her own house in Milwaukie. She has a great sense of humor and is very independent, and needs some assistance as she showers, takes medications, and eats.</p>
<p>You will spend one night a week supporting a woman who lives in her own apartment in Milwaukie. She has a sassy sense of humor and lots of interests. She experiences dementia and balance issues, but does many of the tasks of daily living independently. She needs a little help with personal care and assistance to prevent falls.</p>
<p> </p>
<p><b>HOURS (40 per week):</b></p>
<ul>
<li>8pm on Tuesday to 8am on Wednesday; 6pm on Wednesday to 8am on Thursday; 6pm on Thursday to 8am on Friday.</li>
<li>Any holidays that fall during your regularly scheduled shifts.</li>
</ul><p> </p>
<p><b>TO APPLY:</b></p>
<ul>
<li>Send a resume and cover letter via this webpage.</li>
<li>No calls, please.</li>
<li>UCP is an equal opportunity employer, and actively seeks applicants from diverse backgrounds.</li>
</ul><p><b> </b></p>
<p><b>ABOUT UCP:</b></p>
<ul>
<li>United Cerebral Palsy of Oregon &amp; SW Washington (UCP) was recently ranked as one of the 100 Best Non-Profit Employers in Oregon.</li>
<li>We support adults with all kinds of developmental disabilities, so that they can live, work and play independently.</li>
<li>Find out more about us at: <a href="#URL_7617cd5dd242b308708892628e75daa7c940ab1284b8926867f755eb10ffb752#" rel="nofollow" class="external">#URL_2b35257099321dacb52af707d4a6bdf5dd187d81d6bfd24398f0e5b68a6727a7#</a>.</li>
</ul>

requirements:
<ul>
<li>High school diploma or GED.</li>
<li>At least 18 years old.</li>
<li>Must pass a physical with a lifting test of 75lbs, drug screen, reference check, and criminal history background check.</li>
<li>Experience in developmental disabilities and/or mental health preferred, but not required.</li>
</ul>

benefits:
<ul>

<p><b>IC&amp;E Technician | Bakersfield, CA Mt. Poso</b></p>
<p></p>
<p><b>Principal Duties and Responsibilities: </b></p>
<ul>
<li>Calibrates, tests, maintains, troubleshoots, and installs all power plant instrumentation, control systems and electrical equipment.</li>
<li>Performs maintenance on motor control centers, motor operated valves, generators, excitation equipment and motors.</li>
<li>Performs preventive, predictive and corrective maintenance on equipment, coordinating work with various team members.</li>
<li>Designs and installs new equipment and/or system modifications.</li>
<li>Troubleshoots and performs maintenance on DC backup power equipment, process controls, programmable logic controls (PLC), and emission monitoring equipment.</li>
<li>Uses maintenance reporting system to record time and material use, problem identified and corrected, and further action required; provides complete history of maintenance on equipment.</li>
<li>Schedule, coordinate, work with and monitor contractors on specific tasks, as required.</li>
<li>Follows safe working practices at all times.</li>
<li>Identifies safety hazards and recommends solutions.</li>
<li>Follows environmental compliance work practices.</li>
<li>Identifies environmental non-compliance problems and assist in implementing solutions.</li>
<li>Assists other team members and works with all departments to support generating station in achieving their performance goals.</li>
<li>Trains other team members in the areas of instrumentation, control, and electrical systems.</li>
<li>Performs housekeeping assignments, as directed.</li>

In addition to 47,000 megawatts of generation capacity, enough to supply nearly 40 million homes, our retail electricity providers serve more than two million customers.<br><br><b>GENERAL SUMMARY</b><br><br>The Instrumentation Controls and Electrical Technician (IC&amp;E) is responsible for the maintenance and repair of all instruments, control systems and electrical equipment in the power plant and the associated facilities. The IC&amp;E Technician performs work of high skill in the inspection repair, testing, adjustment, installation and removal of all electronic and electrical power plant equipment and systems.<br><br><b>PRIMARY FUNCTIONS</b><br><br>-Safely perform preventive and corrective maintenance on all plant equipment, systems, and emergency backup systems.<br>-Maintain complete equipment files, logs, and drawings for all designated equipment.<br>-Assist in maintaining and controlling spare parts inventory to maintain plant reliability. Assist in physical inventories.<br>-Participate in the administrative maintenance of the plant operating manuals, technical references, prints and diagrams and preventive/predictive maintenance records.<br>-Inspect repair, install and remove all types of motors, generators, transformers, switches, control equipment, wiring, electrical signal and communication systems, and storage batteries, and work with electrical systems up to and including 4160 volts.<br>-Conduct trouble shooting and analysis on DCS, PLC and GE Mark VI turbine control equipment.<br>-Maintain all plant instrumentation and electrical test equipment.<br>-Fully support and participate in NAES Safety and Regulatory Compliance Programs.<br>-Complete other tasks as assigned by the Maintenance Manager or the Plant Manager.<br><br><b>SCOPE OF RESPONSIBILITY</b><br><br>The IC&amp;E Technician operates from established and well-known procedures under minimum supervision, performing duties independently with only general direction given. Decisions are made within prescribed operating and casualty procedures and guidelines. The ICE Technician encounters recurring work situations of high complexity, with occasional variations from the norm.<br><br>The IC&amp;E Technician must perform work in a safe manner resulting in no accidents, in compliance with all environmental permits at all times. The ICE Technician must effectively communicate information to supervision and fellow plant personnel, relative to the condition of the plant equipment and performance, and provide suggestions for plant improvements. The ICE Technician must perform duties in such a way to result in an optimum cost-benefit ratio.<br><br>The IC&amp;E Technician may also be assigned the additional responsibility of Maintenance Coordinator/Planner for the site. This is a collateral assignment given to a selected maintenance department technician to assist the Operations and Maintenance Manager with the routine planning and coordination of the site equipment maintenance.<br><br><b>The duties include:</b><br><br>-The planning and review of the preventative, predictive and pro-active type maintenance.<br>-A more in-depth knowledge and ability to use the CMMS system to provide the necessary review and planning to support the O&amp;M Manager in weekly work routines.<br>-Ability to provide the cognitive coordinated efforts necessary to ensure that weekly routines and preventative maintenance are scheduled and documented as complete when the respective maintenance technician indicates that the work has been performed.<br>-Provide the coordination efforts necessary in support of allowing the O&amp;M Manager to provide the necessary supervision and direction of the maintenance technicians.<br><br>All employees have the responsibility to both the customer and their co-workers to do the job right the first time and to ensure the customers' needs are being met.<br><br></p>

S089  |  Office Manager
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
company_profile: <p>Judicata is building the most advanced legal research, analytics and artificial intelligence tools in the world. Our focus on accuracy and innovation is matched only by our commitment to hiring the best.</p>
<p>We’re a small team of twelve members with engineering and legal backgrounds spanning from Google to the United States Court of Appeals, and we’ve partnered with two of the most visionary investors in Silicon Valley — Peter Thiel and Khosla Ventures.</p>
<p>Ours is a uniquely collaborative environment that fuses engineering, law, product and design. Building an ontology of the law requires a deep understanding of natural and legal language. We leverage advanced language technologies and legal experience to organize the law in ways previously only dreamed of.</p>

description:
<p>Judicata is looking for an Office Manager.</p>
<p>I’m not referring to an administrator or office coordinator or even an executive assistant. Admins can work plug-&amp;-play in nearly any environment, helping a team or department with simple administrative tasks. Their skills are real, their role is necessary, and I’m sure many of them excel at their jobs.  And we don’t need one of them. An Office Manager, on the other hand, takes responsibility for facilitating all the operations of an office. They will be a central hub that is essential to our success. Their job, in effect, is to work often independently and in the background—identifying problems, considering all contributing factors, and executing effective solutions as quickly as possible.</p>
<p>That may not seem like a huge distinction, but maybe it’s easier to illustrate the differences not in terms of what you do as an Office Manager, but what you gain from being one:</p>
<ul>
<li>As an Office Manager you will learn (and hopefully help form) the inner workings of a company. Startups around here are known for openness, transparency and flat hierarchies, but most employees will never actually engage with the organizational machinery that drives the success of the company. You will live in it. As an Office Manager, you join the Operations department, tasked with understanding the ways in which we work, supporting our colleagues when possible, and improving our work processes.</li>
<li>As an Office Manager you will contribute directly to the creation of an incredible product. We’re tackling big problems here, and to achieve our ambitious goals we’ve recruited exceptional talent in engineering and law and have enlisted the support and guidance from some notable investors. The candidate we hire for this position will be a highly valued member of our small team; they will see the effects of their contributions in real-time; and they will feel crucial because we very much need them right now.</li>
<li>As an Office Manager you can jumpstart your career. This seems like an unlikely path to success, but I speak from experience when I say that it is a lesser-known road. I cut my professional teeth as an Office Manager in the early days at Box, and the skills I gained there have propelled me in my career. Working in Operations, especially in the beginning stages of a startup, grants access to every area of the business—administration, finance, human resources, recruiting, business development, marketing and sales, support, organizational development, strategy, et cetera. For the right individual who is eager to work, learn, and grow in their career, immersing daily in this myriad of areas provides a rare experience and invaluable education in how to successfully run a business. As a former Office Manager, I have become an organizational jack-of-all-trades and have come to realize that there is significant value in knowing how to get things done in a variety of areas.</li>
</ul><p>It’s impossible to articulate just what all we need from you, but here is a general list of responsibilities that begins to scratch the surface:</p>
<ul>
<li>Oversee all daily aspects of office facilities
<ul>
<li>Welcome and receive guests</li>
<li>Manage lunch/dinner ordering</li>
<li>Manage all grocery ordering/shopping</li>
<li>Manage all supply ordering</li>
<li>Handle all postage and shipping (inbound and outbound)</li>
<li>Oversee building management (e.g., repairs, cleaning, improvements, relocation, etc)</li>
<li>Organize and maintain a clean and professional physical space in the office</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>Plan and manage social life for the company
<ul>
<li>e.g., Happy Hours, quarterly off-site events, holiday parties, etc</li>
<li>Manage company calendar online</li>
<li>Coordinate guest visits, usability testing, etc.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>Support executive team</li>
<li>Plan, coordinate, and manage special projects; Possible examples include:</li>
<ul>
<li>Recruiting</li>
<li>Designing accounting processes</li>
<li>Fundraising</li>
<li>Conference/event planning</li>
<li>Community engagement (e.g., blog, social networking, etc)</li>
</ul>
</ul><p>_______________________________________________________________________________________________________</p>
<p>If you’re up for all of this, then we hope you apply below. In order to get to know you right away, we’d like you to answer a couple of questions in the cover letter you submit. First, in no more than seven sentences, please tell us why you’re interested in Judicata and what you might hope to be doing here after two years. Second, in no more than three sentences total, please discuss who your favorite villain is, who your least favorite hero is, and why.</p>
<p>We look forward to hearing from you.</p>
<p>Thanks,<br>Jace<br>Director, Finance &amp; Operations</p>
<p></p>

requirements:
<ul>
<li>Entry-level position; 0-2 years of experience (required)</li>
<li>Bachelor’s degree from a top-tier university (required)</li>
<li>Ability to thrive and execute in a fast-paced, demanding environment</li>
<li>Demonstrated organizational skills</li>
<li>Close attention to detail</li>
<li>Proven record of flexibility, resourcefulness, and creativity</li>
<li>Excellent verbal and written communication skills</li>
<li>Exceptional time management and self-motivation</li>
<li>Positive team-centric attitude</li>
<li>Advanced computer literacy – MS Office (especially Excel), Google Docs </li>
</ul>

<p>           </p>
<h3><b> </b></h3>
<p></p>

description:
<p>We are looking to hire an Account Receivable Specialist who wants to be part of a fast-paced startup company.  The ideal candidate will have had collections experience for an enterprise software-as-a-service (SaaS) company and can work in a fast-paced environment.</p>
<p> </p>
<p>Responsibilities:</p>
<p> </p>
<p> You Will Take Ownership of: </p>
<ul>
<li>Accounts receivable activities by initiating the collection process of overdue accounts, closely monitoring aging accounts, and reconciling A/R to the General Ledger.</li>
<li>Researching customer deductions and coordinating resolution, where appropriate, updating system notes with the status of all collection activity.</li>
<li>Responding to customers’ requests for documentation, which may include (but is not limited to) providing invoice copies and statements. </li>
<li>Customer billing files and ensuring information is complete, accurate and compliant with standard operating procedures.</li>
<li>Researching collection activity and status; following general operating procedures in an effort to recommend amounts for write-off.</li>
<li>Reconciling customer accounts and preparing adjustments.</li>
<li>Ongoing communication with billing staff to resolve accounts receivable issues.</li>
<li>Periodically, preparing reports on the status of accounts receivable to management.  This may include but is not limited to preparing reports for finance and sales management on past due customers and preparing monthly credit summary reports.</li>
<li>Support for monthly accounting close and case reconciliations.</li>
</ul>

requirements:
<p>Qualifications:</p>
<ul>
<li>A Bachelor’s degree, preferably in Accounting.</li>
<li>At least 3 years of experience in Accounts Receivable.</li>
<li>Experience working in a fast-paced, results-oriented, hands-on environment.</li>
<li>Experience working in a SaaS company.</li>
<li>General understanding of accounting principles.</li>
<li>Expertise using QuickBooks.</li>
<li>Basic knowledge of A/R policies and procedures.</li>
<li>Familiarity of fundamental collection ethics and procedures.</li>
<li>Solid experience managing through customer issues in a friendly, courteous, timely and professional manner.</li>
<li>The ability to collaborate with others internally, and consistently display a “team player” attitude.</li>

<p><b>QA Manager (Cable)</b></p>
<p><b>Job Summary</b>  </p>
<p>As a member of the Service Provider Engineering team, you will be responsible for managing a lab and team of Test Engineers to qualify Cable products. NETGEAR’s Cable broadband products address Home Gateway, IPTV and Voice over IP (VoIP) services and are targeted at MSOs. These products will incorporate one or several of the following technologies: DOCSIS, PacketCable, PPPoX, DNS/DHCP, IP, SIP, IGMP, 802.11 Wireless, Ethernet, VPN, Firewalls.  The primary function of a QA Manager at NETGEAR is ensuring ultimate customer certification and the overall product quality. . Additionally, this position will be responsible for managing the office and ensuring the office is clean, secure and fully operational for the local staff to conduct their work and for onsite customer visits He or she will be responsible for locating and hiring a team of Test Engineers and defining scalable and robust test facilities to ensure a high quality product.</p>
<p><b>Job Responsibilities</b></p>
<ul>
<li>Manage the Cable Lab. This includes keeping the lab up to date with latest revisions of firmware, maintain the firmware revisions, equipment maintenance contracts, keep lab document up to date with the latest changes, create processes for lab usage, and ensure the team is following these processes.</li>
<li>Build the lab for cable CPE product qualification that meets all major NA MSOs requirements.</li>
<li>Support the SPBUs international cable sales as required</li>
<li>Hire a team of test engineering to define and execute the test cases per product requirements.</li>
<li>Build and Lead the Test Engineering team to create test plans, test cases, and automate test cases for products as requested.</li>
<li>Manage the work-load for test engineers and prioritize as needed.</li>
<li>Provide schedules for work requests and lead the team to deliver on those schedules.</li>
<li>Lead a team of Test Engineers that will:</li>
</ul><p>◦       Review test plans, test cases, test lab equipment, and verify test coverage.</p>
<p>◦       Perform product testing to verify product performance and diagnose product defects.</p>
<p>◦       Perform verification and regression testing for bug fixes. Log, track and drive to closure all bugs directly with overseas developers.</p>
<p>◦       Develop test plans from requirements and supervise QA testing both within our company and in overseas supplier’s labs.</p>
<p>◦       Set up and maintain in-house Cable Video, DOCSIS, PacketCable and IPTV Network test beds for multiple service provider environments</p>
<p>◦       Monitor/Manage qualification of products with customers QA lab personnel</p>
<p>◦       Understand customer requirements and test plans</p>
<p>◦       Create additional test cases as required to cover customer’s requirements.</p>
<p>◦       Review customer test plans, and integrate into the master test plan.</p>
<p>◦       Review industries new standards, and create a plan to integrate new standards into the lab test capabilities.</p>
<p>◦       Send regular Test Status Reports to product team with metrics to indicate overall test progress and product maturity.</p>
<p>◦       Provide input for manuals, help files, application notes and tech support web pages</p>
<p>◦       Support any 3rd level technical escalations from our technical support. Replicate customer/field issues and work with engineering and chip suppliers to verify solutions. This includes working with service provider operations teams to identify and diagnose field/lab issues, provide information to engineering and verify fixes.</p>
<p>◦       Prepare service provider lab qualification documentation for new products entering certification as well as additional FW releases</p>
<p>◦       Evaluate new products and reference designs.</p>
<ul>
<li>Office Management: Handle inbound and outbound correspondence, keep office supplies stocked, create/maintain security badges, support safety/legal compliance efforts, work with outside vendors if necessary and communicate with the Corporate Facilities and HR teams.</li>
<li>Make the lab a clean and safe environment for Test Engineers.</li>
</ul><p><b> </b></p>

requirements:
<p><b>Skills and Requirements</b></p>
<ul>
<li>Intimately familiar with all major CMTS (Cisco, Arris, Motorola, CASA)</li>
<li>Knowledge of existing MSO testing procedures and personnel and methods</li>
<li>In depth knowledge of PacketCable specifications including #URL_7873ea313220abcb145fe03e78a77af55b3d6ba8c643540ae850475459584ecc#. Candidate will be expected to be able to setup and configure Softswitch, perform voice quality testing etc</li>
<li>Strong understanding of IP networking, TCP/IP, HTTP, FTP, DNS, SNMP and other IP communication protocols. Knowledge of routers, Wireless (802.11x), VPN.</li>
<li>5 years in QA or System Test in a lead role testing networking products, Management experience preferred.</li>
<li>Well-versed in QA methodologies and Test Driven Development.</li>
<li>Experience using test tools, such as Test Director, WinRunner, QAPartner, etc.</li>
<li>Hands-on test capability, operate network test equipment, build test environments, troubleshoot networks and products</li>
<li>Ability to independently track and resolve issues.</li>
<li>Teamwork. This position requires a close working relationship with the extended NETGEAR team, including engineering, operations, sales, customer support, marketing and program management</li>
<li>Multi-tasker. Experience working in a start-up environment.</li>
<li>Good communication skills, both written and verbal.</li>
<li>BS in Computer Science or Engineering.</li>
<li>Knowledge of DSL standards and industry is a plus.</li>
<li>Fluency in Mandarin is a plus.</li>
</ul>

benefits:
<p><b>What Our Company Offers:</b></p>
<ul>
<li>Broad responsibility, autonomy and visibility in a Design Engineering role.</li>
<li>In-depth exposure to real-world customer issues on a global basis across a broad set of applications and products.</li>
<li>Small-company feel in a growth environment</li>
</ul><p><b> </b></p>

================================================================================

S102  |  Marketing Database Decision Strategy Consultant 
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
company_profile: (blank)

description:
<p>Looking for a modeling manager who is self-starting and proactive, wants to have an impact and own end to end results of their work. This modeling manager will be responsible for managing a team that develops predictive marketing models for Small Business credit product campaigns across multiple channels. Routine tasks will involve staff management and hands-on model development, technical documentation, oversight of model reviews and model implementation, and daily collaboration with the data and analytics teams. </p>
<p>The modeling manager must be very familiar with statistical concepts that pertain to predictive models. Broader, practical knowledge of applied statistics, experimental design, and data mining would also be important. He or she will employ multiple databases across multiple data sources with different time period to obtain small business data with various levels of detail, to develop, validate, and oversee development of models that predict events along the credit product acquisition cycle. </p>
<p>Regular communication with business partners will be required. The modeling manager must be comfortable in the role of advocate for the business, when seeking approvals from legal and compliance personnel for new or revised marketing models, when making recommendations to senior management, and with occasional opportunities to share information across the company. Adaptability to a changing legal and compliance environment is imperative. </p>
<p><b>Basic Qualifications </b></p>
<p>10+ years marketing analytics and predictive modeling experience. </p>
<p><b>Minimum Qualifications </b></p>
<p>Masters degree in statistics, operations research, or another field of applied statistics.</p>
<p>10+ years of hands-on statistical applications in the financial services, consumer products marketing, or another related business area.</p>
<p>Experience with campaign execution and database marketing.</p>
<p>Demonstrated creativity with problem solving skills.</p>
<p>Proficient with the SAS BASE and STAT modules, SAS macro language, and SQL.</p>
<p>Practical experience with logistic regression models and scorecards.</p>
<p>Ability to design and provide information for model reporting processes.</p>
<p>Skill with preparing highly detailed model-related documentation, which would be subject to several levels of approval.</p>
<p>Demonstrated ability to share analytical results effectively to non-modelers, including management, law and compliance groups, and others.</p>
<p>Ability to manage a predictive modeling team as a hands-on contributor.</p>
<p>Embraces teamwork and collaboration, and is regarded by colleagues as a respected authority in applied statistics.</p>
<p>Must be flexible, able to thrive, and meet stringent deadlines, in a rapidly changing, fast paced environment.</p>
<p>Ability to adapt new environment quickly with change management experience Strong oral, written, and interpersonal communication skills.</p>

requirements:
<p><b>Preferred Skills </b></p>
<p>Experience with SAS E-Miner or Xeno, or another model building tool.</p>
<p>Previous management of an analytics team is preferred but not necessary.</p>
<p>Familiarity of databases like Hemisphere or BMG.</p>

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
company_profile: <p>We are not your average Monday mail recruiters. We are here to align stars and connect dots, not just match titles with positions &amp; salary demands with salary offerings. Our approach is simple; we read between the lines to see YOU. Both of you. Employer and employee. You &amp; Them is the most personal, innovative and open-minded professional recruiting can be. A creative community of great minds who seek minds that think alike.</p>
<p><b>You &amp; Them is Us.</b><br> Real people. Nice to meet you.</p>

description:
<p><b>ABOUT US</b></p>
<p>Narrative, founded April 2013, is a marketing, entertainment and technology collaborative based in New</p>
<p>York. We have the privilege of working with great partners like Under Armour, Samsung, TIME Inc., Coty</p>
<p>Fragrances, Universal Pictures, RushCard, Bombay Sapphire, and more.</p>

<p>Narrative is looking for a Copywriter to join our team in New York.  We are looking for people who:</p>
<p>● are proactive</p>
<p>● takes initiative</p>
<p>● are comfortable with working closely and collaboratively across the entire agency</p>
<p>● aren’t afraid to stretch themselves</p>
<p>● are willing to go above and beyond the call of duty</p>
<p></p>
<p>We are a start up shop in build mode, and we’re looking for master craftspeople that want to teach and learn from one another. As a Copywriter, you have a way with words and a superb ability to harness the power of narrative to provoke thoughts, emotions, and responses. You are a bibliophile, a writer who thinks strategically and understands that content is king, but only through meaningful exchanges with the audience. You cringe at the phrase “more than what words can describe”…you hate it, despise it, and that’s why we want you. With this role, you will report directly to the Head of Strategy + Creative Development. Your day to day will consist of working closely with our strategists, designers, account persons and clients, to ideate and create compelling concepts and content.</p>

requirements:

<p>● Research brand, products, services, consumers and the marketplace to ensure strategic alignment and that the copy/concepts are on target with objectives.</p>
<p>● Present material to internal/client teams in a professional, comprehensive and inspiring manner.</p>
<p>● Manage multiple projects, working with project management to ensure all deadlines are met.</p>
<p>● Edit and proofread materials for typos, grammar, and to ensure consistent and accurate tone of voice.</p>
<p></p>
<p><b>YOU ARE THE FUTURE</b></p>
<p>● Understand the potential of existing and emerging industry standards and practices.</p>
<p>● Keep on top of what is going on around you in the field of digital, marketing, PR, and design.</p>
<p>● Have a deep understanding of our clients and their consumers</p>
<p>● Identify and create opportunities with brands/3rd party vendors.</p>
<p></p>
<p><b>REQUIRED SKILLS</b></p>
<p>● Minimum of 3 years experience in copywriting and concepting.</p>
<p>● Experience with Keynote, MS Office, Google Doc.</p>
<p>● Passionate and meticulous writer, with a keen eye for detail.</p>
<p>● Well organized with excellent verbal, written and communication skills.</p>
<p>● Have a proven ability to create copy for a wide variety of brands and audiences.</p>
<p>● Ability to turn around quality work while under tight deadlines.</p>
<p>● Proven team player in a collaborative environment with the ability to work independently and effectively.</p>
<p>● Excellent creative, conceptual and critical thinking abilities.</p>
<p>● Able to rationalize and sell concepts/ideas.</p>
<p>● Understand the importance of copy in relations to visual design.</p>
<p>● Ability to take direction, propose new concepts and follow processes.</p>

<p>● Proficiency with a variety of writing styles</p>

<p><b>Key Accountabilities</b></p>
<ul>
<li>Setting up, configuring, testing, deploying and supporting Upstream’s SMS gateways in order to establish SMS connectivity between Upstream and its clients for the purposes of large-scale mobile marketing campaigns.</li>
<li>Collaborating with several internal and external technical teams in order to deliver the desired connectivity inline with other project requirements</li>
<li>Serving as the point of escalation for any SMS connectivity related problems for live projects</li>
<li>Participating in troubleshooting sessions with client technical teams</li>
<li>Proactively administering Upstream’s SMS Gateways in order to ensure the highest level of availability</li>
</ul><p></p>

requirements:

<p><b>Knowledge, Skills and Experience</b></p>
<ul>
<li>University degree in Computer Science, Engineering or similar discipline</li>
<li>5+ years of hands-on experience with Linux/Unix systems and networking technologies, such as VPNs and switched VLANs.</li>
<li>Very good knowledge of TCP/IP, proven experience troubleshooting network connections</li>
<li>Solid experience of commonly used telecommunications protocols for exchanging SMS messages, including SMPP and HTTP</li>
<li>Hands-on experience with SMS Gateways, SMSCs and other mobile operator infrastructure components</li>
<li>Good programming concepts knowledge: algorithms, data structures, network protocols.</li>
<li>Excellent written and verbal skills in English</li>
</ul><p><b>Other Considerations</b></p>
<p>Preference will be given to candidates, who in addition to the above requirements have the following qualifications:</p>
<ul>
<li>Experience in working closely with Mobile Network Operators and associated third parties to set up and troubleshoot integration projects.</li>
<li>Hands-on experience with programming, preferably in Java or Python</li>

<p> <b>Personal Characteristics</b></p>
<ul>
<li>Excellent communication skills.</li>
<li>Demonstrated troubleshooting and problem solving skills</li>
<li>Self-driven with ability to take ownership of issues and follow through to resolution</li>
<li>Strong team player with excellent communication skills. Must be dependable, reliable, and have attention to detail.</li>
<li>Must demonstrate the ability to multitask and thrive in a dynamic environment and the ability to balance and prioritize activities to ensure critical items are addressed.</li>
</ul><p> </p>

In addition to 47,000 megawatts of generation capacity, enough to supply nearly 40 million homes, our retail electricity providers serve more than two million customers.<br><br><b>GENERAL SUMMARY</b><br><br>The Instrumentation Controls and Electrical Technician (IC&amp;E) is responsible for the maintenance and repair of all instruments, control systems and electrical equipment in the power plant and the associated facilities. The IC&amp;E Technician performs work of high skill in the inspection repair, testing, adjustment, installation and removal of all electronic and electrical power plant equipment and systems.<br><br><b>PRIMARY FUNCTIONS</b><br><br>- Safely perform preventive and corrective maintenance on all plant equipment, systems, and emergency backup systems.<br>- Maintain complete equipment files, logs, and drawings for all designated equipment.<br>- Assist in maintaining and controlling spare parts inventory to maintain plant reliability. Assist in physical inventories.<br>- Participate in the administrative maintenance of the plant operating manuals, technical references, prints and diagrams and preventive/predictive maintenance records.<br>- Inspect repair, install and remove all types of motors, generators, transformers, switches, control equipment, wiring, electrical signal and communication systems, and storage batteries, and work with electrical systems up to and including 4160 volts.<br>- Conduct trouble shooting and analysis on DCS, PLC and GE Mark VI turbine control equipment.<br>- Maintain all plant instrumentation and electrical test equipment.<br>- Fully support and participate in NAES Safety and Regulatory Compliance Programs.<br>- Complete other tasks as assigned by the Maintenance Manager or the Plant Manager.<br><br><b>SCOPE OF RESPONSIBILITY</b><br><br>The IC&amp;E Technician operates from established and well-known procedures under minimum supervision, performing duties independently with only general direction given. Decisions are made within prescribed operating and casualty procedures and guidelines. The ICE Technician encounters recurring work situations of high complexity, with occasional variations from the norm.<br><br>The IC&amp;E Technician must perform work in a safe manner resulting in no accidents, in compliance with all environmental permits at all times. The ICE Technician must effectively communicate information to supervision and fellow plant personnel, relative to the condition of the plant equipment and performance, and provide suggestions for plant improvements. The ICE Technician must perform duties in such a way to result in an optimum cost-benefit ratio.<br><br>The IC&amp;E Technician may also be assigned the additional responsibility of Maintenance Coordinator/Planner for the site. This is a collateral assignment given to a selected maintenance department technician to assist the Operations and Maintenance Manager with the routine planning and coordination of the site equipment maintenance.<br><br><b>The duties include:</b><br><br>- The planning and review of the preventative, predictive and pro-active type maintenance.<br>- A more in-depth knowledge and ability to use the CMMS system to provide the necessary review and planning to support the O&amp;M Manager in weekly work routines.<br>- Ability to provide the cognitive coordinated efforts necessary to ensure that weekly routines and preventative maintenance are scheduled and documented as complete when the respective maintenance technician indicates that the work has been performed.<br>- Provide the coordination efforts necessary in support of allowing the O&amp;M Manager to provide the necessary supervision and direction of the maintenance technicians.<br><br>All employees have the responsibility to both the customer and their co-workers to do the job right the first time and to ensure the customers' needs are being met.</p>

<p><b>Project-based Management Consultant</b></p>
<p>Talent Response works to connect independent professionals like yourself that have had it with the 70-hour, 70%-travel work-week with boutique consulting firms trying to better manage variable project demand. <br><br>We are trusted advisers to boutique consulting firms, sourcing the most qualified consultants to augment staff on projects such as M&amp;A due diligence, go-to-market strategy, and litigation support. Unlike consulting job boards, we present only qualified opportunities to you, dramatically reducing your search costs (e.g. marketing, sales) for consulting engagements. After the drought of work, with time often spent drafting unsolicited proposals, the big jobs would finally come in. Like other boutique consulting firms, the firm would struggle to staff up on projects that came in waves. The true cost of this unpredictable project demand extended beyond the cost of lower overall utilization to include costs of employee turnover, layoffs, and deferred project work.</p>
<p>On the other side of the equation, post-MBA independent consultants have precisely the skills required to deliver on those “peak demand” engagements. Increasingly, professionals are rejecting the trade-off between the 70-hour work week and exiting the workforce. Instead, they are utilizing their skills in a way that gives them a bit more control over the nature and intensity of the projects they take on.</p>
<p>Why shouldn't there be a way to meet project surges at consulting firms with independent consultants carrying a management consulting toolkit? Why can’t we better balance the two sides of this equation?</p>
<p>Our answer to that question is Talent Response. Built through partnerships with MBA alumni associations and elite consulting firms, the Talent Response network is comprised of candidates specifically recruited to meet the needs of elite consulting firms. These individuals are independent by design, not by default. Over 80% of our network has earned MBAs from Top 15 schools, and the majority has experience managing project teams at Big Three and boutique consulting firms.</p>
<p>Consulting firms that adopt this approach experience a steadier workload for full-time staff, lower attrition, and fewer lost or deferred projects.  The domain expertise of our professionals can also be leveraged to extend into adjacent practice areas. Our consultative approach – by consultants for consultants – ensures not just a skillset fit but also a cultural fit with the firm. Let's re-imagine consulting together.</p>

<p>*           Capable of working all hours/shifts as needed</p>
